# Getting Started

## Standardized Task Name: Welcome to vProMedia!

**Section:** Getting Started

### Trigger Phrases & Jira Aliases
To ensure the vector database captures all semantic variations, this task may be requested in a Jira card as:

- Fulfill a Production Order
- Start the production workflow for a Spot
- Assign a Dubber to a PO
- Complete the fulfillment for a PRF
- Submit production on a QO
- Manage a Client account
- Merge Client records
- Update Account information for an Advertiser
- Add files to a Client Profile
- Search for a Spot

**Section:** Getting Started

### Trigger Phrases & Jira Aliases

- Fulfill a Production Order

### Task Objective

There are no suggestions because the search field is empty.   vCreative Knowledge Base   vProMedia   Getting Started   General   Resources   Announcements   Archived vPromotions Release Notes   Archived vPPO Release Notes   Archived vProMedia Release Notes   vPPO   FAQ   vPPO

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/1500000936201-general-workflow`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/1500000936201-general-workflow`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
5. Once a request is submitted, the production workflow will begin. This workflow is setup during consultation with the local point-of-contact when on-boarding at the beginning of vPro use and is customized to the expectations of each firm.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
6. Your forms require some basic information to allow the workflow to progress organically. Once submitted the request will require actions to be performed in order to advance to the next steps. For instance, if your firm requires approval from the director of sales before production can begin, then other actions on the request will be blocked until that approval has been met. After production is completed the request may require client/advertiser approval before it can advance to be scheduled and loaded into master control.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
7. For instance, in a TV station workflow, a videographer may need to show completion of their task in order for a status to change from "Needs Filming" to "Post-Production". A request in "Ad Review" status will require an approver to mark the spot as approved before it will advance to "Needs Scheduling". Finally, once a spot has been scheduled it may advance to "Needs Billing" and when that job is marked as completed by checking the "Billed" checkbox, the request will then move to archived.
   Visual Context: Approve and Deny action buttons are shown inline within the order list row or at the top of the order detail view. Approve is green, Deny/Reject is red.


**Referenced Screenshots:**
- ![vCreative_Logo_RGB.png]](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative_Logo_RGB.png?width=200&amp;height=38&amp;name=vCreative_Logo_RGB.png)
- ![Screenshot](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/image-png-Sep-08-2025-09-00-31-5540-PM.png?width=1237&amp;height=692&amp;name=image-png-Sep-08-2025-09-00-31-5540-PM.png)

- ![Screenshot](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/image-png-Sep-08-2025-09-02-10-6655-PM.png?width=1235&amp;height=692&amp;name=image-png-Sep-08-2025-09-02-10-6655-PM.png)
- ![Screenshot](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/image-png-Sep-08-2025-09-03-06-9053-PM.png?width=1231&amp;height=692&amp;name=image-png-Sep-08-2025-09-03-06-9053-PM.png)

   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'in-line_editing.gif' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.

   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'Approvals.gif' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
6. The following are dependant on what role you fulfill, but our roles are very customizable should your job require one of these options and you don't currently have it. Just reach out to our Help Desk!
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
7. The various jobs required to complete a request as assigned to a filtered to only staff who's own accounts are enabled to do that task. Someone who is only a Writer can be assigned to handle Traffic or Scheduling, for example. The POC column will show you a drop-down menu for any job your account has the authority to delegate. You can also delegate through the Context Menu .
   Visual Context: A search bar is positioned at the top of the production order list view, with a magnifying glass icon on the left side of the input field.

   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.

   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.

   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
11. As a manager, you sometimes have to "fix" someone else's errors. While you can click on the client name to open a request back up in the Quick Order view, some fields will be open for you to edit directly from the List View.. Which fields you can edit will depend on your role, but no matter what role, if you can edit the field it will simultaneously lock out other users to avoid and confusion.
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
12. Typically it is the AE, or through them the Advertiser, that will give approvals on work. However, managers do have the option to Approve or Deny these reviews should they be aware of its outcome.
   Visual Context: Approve and Deny action buttons are shown inline within the order list row or at the top of the order detail view. Approve is green, Deny/Reject is red.

   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
7. This is useful to firms that use a central email for production requests where multiple people need to be notified of the activity in the central account. Users can now add alias email addresses to their primary account to receive the same notifications. This feature is beneficial for firms utilizing a central email for production requests, ensuring multiple team members are informed of account activity.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
8. Your primary email address will always be used to login to your account (1)
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
9. The Additional Emails can be separated by a comma and will receive any notifications set to be sent to the primary email. (2)
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
10. Turn on/off Pop up calendar to either select dates from a calendar or enter dates into date fields manually
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
11. Hide uploaded spots in list views if you have digital duties and do not wish to see orders once they've been uploaded to the digital platform
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
12. SELECT FILE OPTIONS
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
13. By default files will be listed by what type of file is attached. (i.e. Final Spot, Copy Points, Final Image Ad) If hovered over with your mouse, you'll see the original file name.
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
14. Show the upload person and timestamp of a file added
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
15. SELECT VOICE REQUESTS
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
16. ON SUBMIT GO TO
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).

- Approve multiple PRF orders

### Task Objective

There are no suggestions because the search field is empty.   vCreative Knowledge Base   vProMedia   Getting Started   General   Resources   Announcements   Archived vPromotions Release Notes   Archived vPPO Release Notes   Archived vProMedia Release Notes   vPPO   FAQ   vPPO

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360052646573-tasks-and-notifications`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360052646573-tasks-and-notifications`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip1.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Every user will get Task and Notification reminders when specific events occur. Most of these events can be set in your user settings for getting emails or in-system notifications about. It is typically in the best interest of the user to stay as informed as possible about workflow events. Managers may also opt to lock certain settings to prevent anyone from being out of the loop. Simply click the "Clipboard" or "Mail" icons in the upper-right to open the Task and Notification panel.
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
6. If your account is set up where you are someone who can be assigned as a POC (Sales, Production, Writing, etc.) the Task notifications tell you when your attention, talent, or services are needed. Each task or job assigned to you may generate a notification that can be clicked on to navigate to the request you are now a POC on.
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
7. For any workflow event that you've enabled an in-system notification for, you will receive that notice in real-time as it occurs. Just like tasks, you can click on the notification event to instantly navigate to a focused view or "Quick Filter" on the relevant request(s).
   Visual Context: A search bar is positioned at the top of the production order list view, with a magnifying glass icon on the left side of the input field.
8. Every Task and Notification also has a Read and Unread state, much like emails. This helps you stay on top of which you have previously clicked on (Read) or which you've yet to investigate (Unread). All unread notifications show up in bold, while the ones you've already clicked on have faded text. You'll also find an "x" icon to the right for clearing individual notifications out if they've already been read/clicked on. You'll also find sorting and Bulk Action menus at the top for Tasks and Notifications.
   Visual Context: A search bar is positioned at the top of the production order list view, with a magnifying glass icon on the left side of the input field.

- Fulfill a Production Order
- Start the production workflow for a Spot

- Complete the fulfillment for a PRF

- Bulk update multiple Production Orders
- Take Bulk Actions on multiple QOs
- Update dates on multiple Spots at once
- Approve multiple PRF orders
- Manage a Client account

### Task Objective

There are no suggestions because the search field is empty.   vCreative Knowledge Base   vProMedia   Getting Started   General   Resources   Announcements   Archived vPromotions Release Notes   Archived vPPO Release Notes   Archived vProMedia Release Notes   vPPO   FAQ   vPPO

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360056305914-voice-requests`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360056305914-voice-requests`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip0.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Asking for voice work on a production request is started through the "Add Voice" link found in the Files column. Typically, only producers or Production Managers have access to this option but exceptions can be made when needed. Groups of voice talent are most often set up as "Voice Pools" during the roll-out and training period. New groups and pools can be established with assistance from the vC support team.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
6. Each voice request can be one of three types:
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
7. Starting a voice request will ask for basic information, such as when the talent should have their parts completed and what part of the script they are reading. Voice requests can be done directly, as an assignment, or as more open requests.
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
8. The "Search Type" defaults to "Talent" which allows you to select the specific talent you wish to voice a part of the script. If you know exactly who you want to use wish to use the search tool to locate the user by category.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
9. The filter or search option is always there if you aren't quite sure which talent to pick. This is used mostly when you have chosen a "Talent Pool" that you are unfamiliar with or is perhaps not located at your physical location or building. You can filter based on many voice qualities and properties. You even have the option to play the talent's sample audio to make sure they're the best fit. Once you found the right voice, simply check them off and click "Select Talent"
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
10. The audition is put out to a voice pool just like the All Swim, however, it allows for multiple talents to upload their voices in hopes of landing the part. This option is mostly used when producers or the advertisers are needing to be extra particular about getting just the right voice and would like to hear staff "try out".
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.

- ![vCreative_Logo_RGB.png]](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative_Logo_RGB.png?width=200&amp;height=38&amp;name=vCreative_Logo_RGB.png)
- ![mceclip0.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip0-Mar-08-2023-02-15-56-3946-PM.png?width=374&amp;height=488&amp;name=mceclip0-Mar-08-2023-02-15-56-3946-PM.png)

5. Users with the ability to do voice-over work should start by making sure they have their Voice Profile set. This is done from the Account section of the user profile. If you do not see this section in your profile, this means you do not currently have the voice ability. You can call or chat the vCreative Help Desk to get this added to your account.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
6. Demo File: Upload a short demo sampling your voice styles
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
7. I am available for voice requests: This needs to be checked for you to be available in the voice pool. Your name will not show as an option if this is not selected.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
8. Include in All Swim: Selecting this will also send you notifications when someone has requested a voice through an All Swim. (An All Swim is a request for a voice with specific attributes. All users with voice abilities that match those attributes in your voice profile will receive an alert to grab the All Swim voice request.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
9. Select Tone: all that match your particular read styles.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.


**Referenced Screenshots:**
- ![vCreative_Logo_RGB.png]](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative_Logo_RGB.png?width=200&amp;height=38&amp;name=vCreative_Logo_RGB.png)
- ![vCreative___vPro-Voice_Profile.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/vCreative___vPro-Voice_Profile.png?width=627&amp;height=335&amp;name=vCreative___vPro-Voice_Profile.png)
- ![vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig.webp?width=216&amp;height=36&amp;name=vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig.webp)

### Expected Outcome (Regression Validation)
Voice Talent Notes: Share any notes that may be helpful to determine if your voice is right for the spot. You may also wish to list specific turn-around times for voice parts to be completed.

- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360050535714-order-entry`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Your unique request forms will be customized to the fields and responses your managers dictate. The form will provide the requirements and options needed to process your request. You will notice highlighting of required fields that have not yet been submitted.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
6. Your request form will automatically save as a draft until submitted. A running draft is saved continuously as you supply info, starting with Client Name. You will find all un-submitted orders in the Drafts folder.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
7. A multi-line or multi-spot request can be done easily by using the [ADD REQUEST] on the right column of the request form.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
8. You can't submit until all required fields are provided.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).

2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
5. Achieving happens naturally after Cart/Scheduled/Dubbed/Uploaded processes are completed
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
6. Only delete requests submitted by mistake or as a test/practice
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
7. Informs Traffic & Production that the request has an updated end date
   Visual Context: The Status field is a colored badge or dropdown found in the top metadata section of the order form, next to the order title.
8. A spot has been provided that requires a tag added by an announcer
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
9. The status to set upon a favorable Spot Review. This tells the producers the spot is approved and ready to be entered into the on-air system
   Visual Context: Approve and Deny action buttons are shown inline within the order list row or at the top of the order detail view. Approve is green, Deny/Reject is red.
10. Mostly used when a complete and/or approved script is attached and the spot now needs to be voiced and produced
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
11. NOTE: All entered requests will be seen by traffic if they are Sold spots. The starting statuses for a request directly address Production and their workflow.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
12. The request includes copy points and information needed to create a new script
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
13. Replaced (not available for selection)
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
14. The script has been completed or revised and the submitter set the option to require review before production proceeds
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
15. The spot has been produced or revised and the submitter set the option to review it before it goes on air
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).

   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip0.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. For groups and industries that involve a request having a life-span, the "Extend" process is used to keep it active longer. After the request is initially entered, a contract extension or other need could simply mean what is already being broadcast or used can keep going longer than the original end date.
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
6. The most common use of Extend in the Action Bar is simply updating the End Date to keep something on-air or being active longer than originally planned. As such, the only field that you can edit is the End Date. If multiple requests from the same order and for the same client are being extended, you can also save time entering just one date instead of once-per-request.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360056075633-copy`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.

4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip0.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Using the Action Bar to copy a request is a very simple way to save time by not starting a new request from scratch. This process is most often used for two cases:
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
6. A previous request is very similar to what now needs to be submitted
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
7. Whichever the case, selecting some requests and clicking Copy will make a duplicate of that past request to then be modified.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
8. Clicking on "Finish" will take the requests back to the Quick Order form to be updated. Depending on your group's usage and industry, the copy process will be configured to clear out or forget certain fields. This is established by managers to make sure the new requests being created do not cause confusion in the workflow.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
9. Should multiple requests from different orders and advertisers be copied at once, the Copy tool will stay visible until all copies are processed and submitted. The "Undo" button can also be used to back out of the Copy process.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360054288294-revise`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360054288294-revise`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip0.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. To make sure everyone in the workflow stays informed, the use of Revise on the Action Bar follows a simple path to update a previously submitted request.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
6. Most non-manager users will only be able to alter a request that was already submitted through the Revision process. This makes sure that the correct workflow is followed and all the correct parties are notified of the changes. After selecting a request , the Revise can be clicked to open up the start of the process.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
7. Each request being revised will need a few minimal changes before they can be resubmitted back into the workflow. Clicking the right-arrow will begin the revision for that specific titled request, opening it up in the familiar Quick Order form.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
8. In addition to any changes to other dates, copy, or production information you must supply a new Due Date and post a Blog. The Revision can not be completed without these as a bare minimum. Any new files or changes to the request can be updated while the Quick Order form is open.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
9. If you are revising just one or multiple requests, they will each need to be addressed fully before you can click "Finish" to finalize all changes. The check-mark shows up in the far-right column of the Revise tool for the requests that have at least met the minimum requirements of a new Due Date and accompanying Blog post.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.

   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
5. For those seeking the broadest spectrum of results, starting a search from the All or Inbox folder has traditionally been the go-to choice. However, with our new firm setting, users gain the flexibility to choose their search scope right from the start, even while within a custom folder.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
6. Choose Search Current Folder if you want to keep the search criteria used from the previous search.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
7. Choose Search All Folders If you want to look for spots with different criteria than the previous search.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
8. In addition to these helpful new options, you can now save your searches quicker too!
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360056073553-reject-request`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip1.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Using the Reject option on the Action Bar will allow a manager to send a request or multiple requests back to their submitter. The Reject option will only show up if the active user is a manager, but other accounts can be set up to have this option if needed, just reach out to the vCreative Help Desk with your request.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
6. Clicking Reject will take the selected request(s) into the familiar right-hand panel for the next step. If multiple requests are selected they will all be shown, and same-client requests will be grouped. To reject a request, it will require a quick blog post. Click the right arrow to reject that specific request.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
7. The blog you post should be specific to why the submitted request is being sent back. The "Post & Reject" button will save and post your Blog and return the request as a Draft to the user who submitted it . That user will then need to address the rejection's cause and resubmit.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/5576060767003-script-spot-approvals`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation

4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow

5. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip2.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
6. When a user submits a request they can mark options that make Script Approval or Spot Approval part of the workflow. This simply means that whenever the creative/production staff completes the writing of copy or production of the final spot that someone, typically the client, has to approve the work before it moves forward.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
7. Next, the AE can utilize the "send email" link in the Files column if they supplied the client's email address while submitting the request.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
8. Once word comes back from the client that the script is good to move forward with the AE or submitter of the request simply check the "Script approved" or "Spot approved" box in the Status/Tasks column. The status will automatically advance to "Needs Production" or "Needs Dubbing/Completing/Releasing"** respectively.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).

6. Resources can be logged, assigned, and scheduled in vPro. This means that the physical equipment or locations that need to be scheduled or reserved for production use can be tracked. Each resource can be saved manually or ingested from a CSV file, and includes details about it's value, rate for use and storage location.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
7. The fastest way to create your resources is to import a properly formatted spreadsheet. This example shows how the fields can be used:
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
8. Resources can also be manually created by clicking the "+ New Resource" button and providing the required fields.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
9. If booking for a production order, the booking tool will be available in List View from the PO column. A Production Director or user with special abilities can schedule resources needed for the jobs assigned. AE's will be able to see the calendar of booked resources on their request.
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip0.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
10. Notifications can be setup in your Account Profile under the Notifications tab. Scroll to the bottom and click Edit to turn on Email, In-system or Both! Then choose if there are only specific types of requests or stations that you wish to see these notifications for and Save.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
11. Resource and Equipment Management is an optional setting for any firm and will display on orders based on the Ad Type settings. You will need to determine the roles in your market that you wish to have access to this feature. (Production Director/Local Admin is recommended)
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.


**Referenced Screenshots:**
- ![vCreative_Logo_RGB.png]](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative_Logo_RGB.png?width=200&amp;height=38&amp;name=vCreative_Logo_RGB.png)
- ![mceclip0.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip0-Mar-08-2023-02-18-06-8031-PM.png)
- ![mceclip0.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip0-Mar-08-2023-02-18-10-1580-PM.png)
- ![mceclip1.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip1-Mar-08-2023-02-18-05-3429-PM.png)
- ![mceclip4.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip4-Mar-08-2023-02-18-04-2205-PM.png)

### Expected Outcome (Regression Validation)
Resources can be logged, assigned, and scheduled in vPro. This means that the physical equipment or locations that need to be scheduled or reserved for production use can be tracked. Each resource can be saved manually or ingested from a CSV file, and includes details about it's value, rate for use

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/4402863566235-update-multiple-dates-with-the-bulk-actions-feature`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.

4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'Pic_2.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
6. Update multiple dates with the Bulk Actions feature
   Visual Context: The Status field is a colored badge or dropdown found in the top metadata section of the order form, next to the order title.
7. Previously, updating dates on a request had to be done from each individual request line. Now, if you have multiple requests that will need updating to the same dates (for instance, due dates that need to be pushed back a week or start dates that have been postponed) use the bulk actions function by selecting more than one request.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
8. The bulk actions option will only be available if you have multiple requests selected
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
9. Date fields will auto-fill if they are the same for each request.
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.

5. The navigation in vPro is centered around the left-most column on your screen. 6. The folders it shows will be dependant on what you have set to show. This is done first during the "New Device" wizard but can be easily updated too.

7. In the Cog Menu, you will see a " Folder Options " section, and "Settings" will open a panel that lets you show or hide folders from your left-hand navigation as well as change the default folder you see when first visiting the site.
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.

   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360052401373-folder-options`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360052401373-folder-options`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip2.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. The Folder Options for each user are set once during the "New Device" wizard then can be updated from the Cog > Folder Options > Settings menu options.
   Visual Context: The Status field is a colored badge or dropdown found in the top metadata section of the order form, next to the order title.
6. Here, you can set the Default folder you see when logging in, which folders/filters are shown/hidden in the Left-Hand Navigation and which will show in your Context Menu (right-click menu).
   Visual Context: A search bar is positioned at the top of the production order list view, with a magnifying glass icon on the left side of the input field.
7. The diagonal-cross icon to the left of each folder's name is a handle you can click-and-drag to re-order the folders. The "Reset Folders" link at the bottom restores them to their original order. If you have saved a search result as a custom folder you can rename or delete those here as well.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).


**Referenced Screenshots:**
- ![vCreative_Logo_RGB.png]](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative_Logo_RGB.png?width=200&amp;height=38&amp;name=vCreative_Logo_RGB.png)
- ![mceclip0.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip0-Mar-08-2023-02-15-42-9049-PM.png?width=246&amp;height=227&amp;name=mceclip0-Mar-08-2023-02-15-42-9049-PM.png)
- ![mceclip2.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip2-Mar-08-2023-02-15-43-4938-PM.png?width=301&amp;height=453&amp;name=mceclip2-Mar-08-2023-02-15-43-4938-PM.png)
- ![vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig.webp?width=216&amp;height=36&amp;name=vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig.webp)

### Expected Outcome (Regression Validation)
The Folder Options for each user are set once during the "New Device" wizard then can be updated from the Cog > Folder Options > Settings menu options.

## Standardized Task Name: Right-Click Context Menu

**Section:** Navigation & Views

### Trigger Phrases & Jira Aliases
To ensure the vector database captures all semantic variations, this task may be requested in a Jira card as:

- Fulfill a Production Order
- Start the production workflow for a Spot
- Assign a Dubber to a PO
- Complete the fulfillment for a PRF
- Submit production on a QO
- Copy an existing Spot
- Extend a Campaign's PO
- Revise an existing Quick Order
- Duplicate a QO for a new Campaign
- Create a copy of an Endorsement Spot

### Task Objective

- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/5448168471963-right-click-context-menu`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'SNAG-0000.jpg' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Right-Click Context Menu
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
6. Whether viewing items from the list view, compact view or calendar view, the context menu is available by right-clicking on the request line. The context menu allows you to work faster with less navigation.
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
7. Changing folder views (options available based on settings). To set folders that will display here, open the settings menu in the top right corner next to the resource calendar. Then, select the folders to show in the "Show in Context Menu" column.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
8. Delegating assignments (if the user account has delegation permissions)
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
9. Add more notes associated with the request to the Special Instructions
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
10. All in PO will open the order to an isolated view containing only the requests grouped within 1 order
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
11. Assign resources or equipment to the request or book an appointment
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
12. Edit will open the order to the data entry form
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.

The workflow is complete when the expected UI state change is visible — for example, the order appears in the Production Orders list, a success confirmation message is displayed, or the status badge updates to the new state.

- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360050538374-calendar-view`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.

   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip0.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. The vPro Calendar view enables users to do more while having a 30,000 foot view of your production workload. If the calendar view is enabled in your firm settings, you will be able to switch viewing modes from the Settings icon at the top right of your account.
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip1.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
6. You can select if you want to stay in the List view or change any of your folders/filters to Calendar View to see quickly what requests are due to be completed or which requests are scheduled to start. You can sort by the due date or run dates and Month view or Week view.

   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
8. All details of a request can be displayed from the calendar by clicking on a request and then Show Details.
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
9. From the details of the calendar view you can process approvals, add files, update scripts and more!
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.

# Client & Account Settings

## Standardized Task Name: Notifications

**Section:** Client & Account Settings

### Trigger Phrases & Jira Aliases
To ensure the vector database captures all semantic variations, this task may be requested in a Jira card as:

- Create a new Quick Order (QO)
- Submit a new Spot request
- Enter a new PO for radio commercial
- Start a new Order Entry
- Add a new Production Order
- Submit a Quick Order for a Campaign
- Create a QO for a radio spot
- Enter a new request for an Endorsement Spot
- Submit a new Trade or Liner order
- Start a new Spot for a Client

### Task Objective
Skip to content   Open main navigation Close main navigation   Go to the vCreative home page   Welcome! How can we help?   There are no suggestions because the search field is empty.   vCreative Knowledge Base   vProMedia   Client & Account Settings   General   Resources   Announcements   Archived vPromotions Release Notes   Archived vPPO Release Notes   Archived vProMedia Release Notes   vPPO   F

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360050650033-notifications`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360050650033-notifications`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
5. Click on the drop-down menu next to your name in the upper-right corner of your screen. Select the Notifications tab at the top of the page. You can then click the Edit button to the far-right of each section to control which notifications you want to receive.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
6. Receive this notification when one of your spots (or spots you are assigned to) is placed in the Attention Status.
   Visual Context: The Status field is a colored badge or dropdown found in the top metadata section of the order form, next to the order title.
7. Receive a notification each time a new order is submitted. (Account Executives will only receive notifications for their own requests.)
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
8. Stay connected to an order you submitted for someone else, in case there are any questions or concerns that you need to address.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
9. Aircheck file upload :
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
10. Get notified when a file is added to a spot using the “Aircheck” File Type
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
11. Script file upload :
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
12. Get notified when a file is added to a spot using the “Script” File Type
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
13. Final spot file upload :
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
14. Get notified when a file is added to a spot using the “Final Spot” File Type
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
15. Script approved: Instantly know when a spot in “Script Review” gets approved and is ready for Production.
   Visual Context: Approve and Deny action buttons are shown inline within the order list row or at the top of the order detail view. Approve is green, Deny/Reject is red.
16. Spot approved :
   Visual Context: Approve and Deny action buttons are shown inline within the order list row or at the top of the order detail view. Approve is green, Deny/Reject is red.


**Referenced Screenshots:**
- ![vCreative_Logo_RGB.png]](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative_Logo_RGB.png?width=200&amp;height=38&amp;name=vCreative_Logo_RGB.png)
- ![vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig.webp?width=216&amp;height=36&amp;name=vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig.webp)

### Expected Outcome (Regression Validation)
Receive a notification each time a new order is submitted. (Account Executives will only receive notifications for their own requests.)

### Task Objective

There are no suggestions because the search field is empty.   vCreative Knowledge Base   vProMedia   Client & Account Settings   General   Resources   Announcements   Archived vPromotions Release Notes   Archived vPPO Release Notes   Archived vProMedia Release Notes   vPPO   F

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360051686434-client-management`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/360051686434-client-management`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip1.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Client names in vPro can be imported from a list provided during your initial setup. That list can include contact information which we will map into the fields used in vPro. We can then lock-down the creation of new clients to prevent duplicates if needed. This lock-down is also in effect when we enable integrations with a traffic or business system to allow it to control the Client names available when submitting a Quick Order .
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
6. First, a group can choose not to allow the creation of new Clients without manager approval. If enabled, the QO form will confirm the new client creation when a pre-existing Client Name is not used by the submitter. The order can still be filled out but not fully submitted. The submitter will be notified that it is pending approval and will remain a draft until the managerial approval of the new client/advertiser. The managers instantly get an in-system notification and upon approval, the submitter is notified in return that their saved draft with the new Client can now be submitted. Contact your vCreative support team to enable this option.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
7. For your approved and existing clients, managers can edit the contact details, attach files, and merge incorrect client information into the correct ones. Under “Settings” in the left-hand navigation there is a “Clients” section.
   Visual Context: A file upload drop zone is shown as a dashed-border rectangle in the center of the form, with a cloud upload icon and the text 'Drag files here or click to upload'.
8. 1. Settings
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
9. 2. Clients
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
10. Editing the client lets you update/fix client names as well as general information that hasn’t been collected during the import of clients. Now, your edit includes attaching "Client Files" that are often used over and over for an advertiser. These are then presented on the QO form to be included on a request when needed, perfect for jingles, backgrounds, pronunciations, etc. The File Type available pull from those you already use, new once can always be added at your request.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
11. “Preloaded Files” dropdown on the QO form
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
12. You start by finding the correct client name you wish to use from now on. When you click the "Merge Into" button you are given the option to choose one or multiple incorrect versions of that label. You will be asked to confirm that all selected client names and the orders they are on will henceforth be under the correct client you selected.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.

- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/4402879746331-time-logging`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'Pic_7.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. For adtypes that need to track time spent for each job, vPro provides the Time Logging capabilities that allow your team to record time spent or quantities, such as voiceovers provided, for each request. This is configurable by a local admin (typically a Production Director) under the vPro Ad Types setting in the left-navigation bar. Click on the Time Logging button at the top of the table. In the right slideout you can set up the list of descriptions for each task name that need to be tracked, add instructions like the type of data that needs to be recorded (i.e. hours/minutes spent, quantities of graphics, voiceovers, music tracks etc…) then, choose the type of input fields for those descriptions. Each Task name can also include a rate charged based on the time or quantity entered.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
6. Once a job has been marked completed, the assigned person will choose the Task Name and record their data.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
7. Please contact the Help desk for assistance with getting your adtypes setup for time logging.
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/4415562988059-appointment-pre-booking`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/4415562988059-appointment-pre-booking`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip5.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. SAVE THE DATE!
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
6. New functionality has been added to the Resource and Equipment Management tool that will allow appointments to be added to a request, along with any resources or equipment that might be needed to fulfill the order.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
7. As a Salesperson, you can now pre-book a client appointment with or without attachment to a request on the resource calendar. This allows you to “save-the-date” whether for recording of a client ad, meeting for a consultation or for filming the ad. For instance, if a client is coming to the station to record their own commercial or if your production crew will be filming an ad on location, this feature will help you keep all information about a request in one place.
   Visual Context: A primary action button labeled 'Submit' or 'Save' is located in the bottom-right corner of the order form, rendered in the site's primary color (teal/blue).
8. If the ad type is using the Resource and Equipment feature, you will see a book icon in the PO column of the list view.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
9. Clicking on the Book icon will open the Resource Calendar. From there, you will fill in the required contact information and choose a booking start and end date and time. Then, Save.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
10. Once an appointment has been saved, the approving manager (typically the Production Director or Creative Services Director) will receive a notification that a new booking has been added and is pending approval.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
11. Upon clicking on that notification, the approving director will be taken to a listing of any pending appointment requests.
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
12. On the Appointment page, the approving director will approve or deny each pending request. If a request is denied, a reason must be entered.
   Visual Context: Approve and Deny action buttons are shown inline within the order list row or at the top of the order detail view. Approve is green, Deny/Reject is red.
13. Once approved or denied, the requesting user will receive a notification that this appointment has either been approved and added to the Resource Calendar or if denied, will have a reason the request was denied in the notification center.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.


**Referenced Screenshots:**
- ![vCreative_Logo_RGB.png]](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative_Logo_RGB.png?width=200&amp;height=38&amp;name=vCreative_Logo_RGB.png)
- ![mceclip4.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip4-Mar-08-2023-02-11-48-6617-PM.png)
- ![mceclip5.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip5-Mar-08-2023-02-11-47-2331-PM.png)
- ![mceclip6.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip6-Mar-08-2023-02-11-47-7590-PM.png)
- ![mceclip7.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip7-Mar-08-2023-02-11-46-1386-PM.png)

### Expected Outcome (Regression Validation)

There are no suggestions because the search field is empty.   vCreative Knowledge Base   vProMedia   Advanced Tools & Tips   General   Resources   Announcements   Archived vPromotions Release Notes   Archived vPPO Release Notes   Archived vProMedia Release Notes   vPPO   FAQ

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/5515515923227-how-do-i-use-the-script-template-library-`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/5515515923227-how-do-i-use-the-script-template-library-`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
5. vPro includes more than the ability to archive previous production requests and spots. It can also be the home for frequently used scripts in a template library. The script template library is found under the vPro tab in the left sidebar. Once it’s opened all of the saved templates will be on display. New scripts can be added using the new template button. Naming the script is required to add it to the library. Tags and script category may be added to allow the script to be more easily found in the filter. The filter is located near the top right of the script template library screen. As you type the search will update the list displayed in real time. The actions column allows editing or removal of a script from the library. Clicking on the script template icon, that looks like an eye, will allow you to create a new request from the template by bringing up the script time sidebar. More details about the script timer are available in another video.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
