5. Drafts in vPro will be created when you click the +New Request button. They will save everything you do in an order and the draft will remain in your drafts folder until you delete it. So this makes a great tool to create templates for frequently used requests.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.

Drafts in vPro will be created when you click the +New Request button. They will save everything you do in an order and the draft will remain in your drafts folder until you delete it. So this makes a great tool to create templates for frequently used requests.

- Start a new Order Entry
- Add a new Production Order

- Submit a new Trade or Liner order
- Start a new Spot for a Client

### Task Objective

There are no suggestions because the search field is empty.   vCreative Knowledge Base   vProMedia   Advanced Tools & Tips   General   Resources   Announcements   Archived vPromotions Release Notes   Archived vPPO Release Notes   Archived vProMedia Release Notes   vPPO   FAQ

### Prerequisites / Starting State
- **Article URL:** `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/5448120146331-lead-time-by-ad-type`
- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/5448120146331-lead-time-by-ad-type`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Screenshot shows 'mceclip1.png' — refer to the embedded image for UI layout reference.
5. Lead Time By Ad Type vProMedia
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
6. Submitting a new request always requires a due date. However, the level of work being asked for in the request could better inform the due date.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.
7. Make sure that simple work can be "due tomorrow" while more complex requests adhere to defined rules by the production department. Your order forms can now have set lead times based on the Due Date entered to ensure enough time between production and ready for air.
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
8. Customer Support or Users with Local Admin access* can set the lead time per Ad Type or project from the Local Admin>vPro Ad types tab>Ad type options>Lead Time
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
9. For instance, setting 3 days will ensure that there are 3 business days between the Due date selected on the form and the first available start date. (US holidays and weekends are not included)
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
10. A lead time of 3 days** for this ad type means that selecting a due date of 12-9-2022 will give the first available start date of 12-14-2022.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.
11. *Gaining Local Admin Access requires additional training with a vPro implementation specialist, contact vCreative Support for details.
   Visual Context: A blue or green "+" or "New Request" button is typically located in the top-left header area beneath the vProMedia navigation bar.


**Referenced Screenshots:**

- ![mceclip0.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip0-2.png)
- ![mceclip1.png](https://vcreativeinc.com/hubfs/Knowledge%20Base%20Import/mceclip1-3.png)

The workflow is complete when the expected UI state change is visible — for example, the order appears in the Production Orders list, a success confirmation message is displayed, or the status badge updates to the new state.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/5516379656731-how-do-i-delete-my-drafts-`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.

4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
5. If an order that is saved in the Drafts folder is no longer needed, it can remove by setting the status to "Deleted". Users can also select more than one draft to set multiple statuses to deleted to remove ALL of those requests at once. If an order has already been submitted, meaning traffic and production departments can now see it, a Revision is usually the best way to proceed. This way everyone can be certain that the entire staff knows the request is being halted and didn't advance through the workflow. In case they need to be found at a later date, deleted requests are still visible using the search feature. However, deleted DRAFTS are not searchable. More search information is available in another video.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.

- **System State:** User must be logged into vProMedia/vSuite Platform with appropriate role permissions (e.g., Account Executive, Sales Manager, Production Manager, or Producer depending on the task).
- **Required Data:** Order details, client name, ad type, air dates, and any supporting files should be prepared before beginning.

### Step-by-Step Execution Guide

1. Navigate to `https://vcreativeinc.com/knowledge/hc/en-us/articles/5516048631835-how-can-i-find-out-when-something-was-done-on-a-request-`.
   Visual Context: The vCreative Knowledge Base article page loads with the article title in an `<h1>` heading at the top, followed by body content with numbered steps and embedded screenshots. The left sidebar shows the full vProMedia category navigation.
2. Open main navigation Close main navigation
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
3. Go to the vCreative home page
   Visual Context: Navigation links and action buttons are rendered in the left sidebar (primary navigation) or the top action bar of the vProMedia dashboard.
4. Fulfillment & Production Workflow
   Visual Context: Refer to the screenshot embedded in the knowledge base article for exact UI element placement and visual confirmation.
5. Every request tracks a HISTORY - a record of each update and edit from the moment of creation. This allows users to look through and see who did what and when. Access a request history by using the icon in the PO column. Any changes will have a stricken out version along with what it’s replacing. There are two views available in the history sidebar. You can choose between simple view and full view. Request history also includes a search tool to narrow down what is listed within the current request. You can search by typing in names, dates, and even text from scripts or blog entries. Your recent user history is also available. It can be found in the user account menu on the top right of the screen. If an order leaves your view due to a status change or filtering options, you can quickly recall the most recent activity in your account. Users also have the ability to set a date range or search by user activity to narrow down the history that is being viewed.
   Visual Context: A dropdown menu typically appears inline within the order form, showing a list of Ad Types (e.g., Radio Commercial, TV Spot, Digital). The active selection is highlighted.

- ![vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig](https://vcreativeinc.com/hs-fs/hubfs/vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig.webp?width=216&amp;height=36&amp;name=vCreative-PromoSuite-Email-Sig.webp)

The workflow is complete when the expected UI state change is visible — for example, the order appears in the Production Orders list, a success confirmation message is displayed, or the status badge updates to the new state.
